/************************************************************************
**
** Basic Screen Layout
** screen components.
**
**	HelpRect (Rect)
**		The space allocated to display the rollover help
**	HelpFont (int)
**		Index of the font to use rollover help.
**	BackPos (Point)	
**		The upper-left corner of the "back" item.
**	ContinuePos (Point)
**		The upper-left corner of the "continue" item.
**	BackColor	(Vector)
**		The color to use for background fills.
**		<r, g, b>
**      	Texture 는 "XXX.DTX" File 을 사용한다.
**	BossRect (Rect)
**		The space allocated to display the boss meter
*************************************************************************/

[BasicLayout]
HelpRect	= (150,430,560,470)
HelpFont	= 4 
HelpSize	= 20

BackFont	= 0
BackSize	= 16

MainPos		= (128,390)
BackColor	= <185.0, 205.0, 95.0>

/************************************************************************
**
** Screen Layout
**
** Screen layout 은 Screen 구성하는 특별한 위치와 싸이즈 로 정의 되어있다.
**
**	TitlePos (Point)
**	-The 좌측위코너"corner" of the title string.
**
**	TitleFont (Integer)
**	-Specifices which font to use for the title.
**
**	TitleSize (Integer)
**	-Specifices the size to use for the title.
**
**	PageRect (Rect)
**	-The rectangle used by the screen items.
**	-(left,top,right,bottom)
**
**	ItemSpace (Integer)
**	-The vertical space between menu items.
**
**	FontFace
**	-Specifices which font to use by default for items.
**
**	FontSize
**	-Specifices default size at which to render text.
**
**	MusicIntensity
**	-Specifies what the menu intensity should be for this
**	-screen (1-7, with 1=silence, 7=high action)
*/

/************************************************************************
**
**  Font Layout
**
**
**  Font<N>  (string)
**
**		Path to the TrueType font to use for font N
**
**	FontSize<N> (int)
**
**		Point size to use when creating the font texture for font N. The 
**	higher the value used, the larger the font texture will be (creating
**	cleaner text at the expense of memory. If unspecified a default value
**	of 24 is used.
**
*/

/************************************************************************
**
**	HUD Layout
**
** The HUDLayout definitions specify positions and sizes for in-game
** interface components. If an attribute isn't listed for a particular
** layout, the base value defined in the default layout [HUDLayout0] will
** be used.
** 
** Note:
**		Positions (AmmoBasePos, etc.) are relative to a 640x480 resolution
**			and will be scaled for other resolutions. 
**		Offsets are absolute distances and are not scaled for different
**			resolutions.
**
**
**	LayoutName (Integer)
**
**		The string id used to identify the layout configuration.
**
**	BarHeight (Integer)
**
**		The height used for the HUD bars.
**
**	BarScale (Float)
**
**		The number of pixels per point in the various HUD bars. 
**
**
**	AmmoBasePos (Point)
**
**		Relative position of the Ammo display.
**
**	UseAmmoBar (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides ammo bar, 1 - shows ammo bar 
**
**	AmmoClipOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-RIGHT corner of the ammo-in-clip bar. 
**
**	AmmoBarOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the total ammo bar.
**
**	UseAmmoText (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides ammo text, 1 - shows ammo text 
**
**	AmmoTextOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the ammo text. 
**
**	AmmoIconOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the ammo type icon. 
**
**
**	HealthBasePos (Point)
**
**		Relative position of the Health and Armor display.
**
**	UseHealthBar (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides health bar, 1 - shows health bar 
**
**	HealthBarOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the health bar.
**
**	ArmorBarOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the  upper-left corner of the armor bar.
**
**	UseHealthText (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides health/armor text, 1 - shows health/armor text 
**
**	HealthTextOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the health text. 
**
**
**	ArmorTextOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the armor text. 
**
**	UseHealthIcon (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides health/armor icons, 1 - shows health/armor icons 
**
**	HealthIconOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the health icon. 
**
**	ArmorIconOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the armor icon. 
**
**
**
**	AirBasePos (Point)
**
**		Relative position of the Air display.
**
**	UseAirIcon (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides air icon, 1 - shows air icon
**
**	AirIconOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the air icon. 
**
**	UseAirText (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides air text, 1 - shows air text 
**
**	AirTextOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the air text. 
**
**	UseAirBar (Integer)
**
**		0 - hides air bar, 1 - shows air bar 
**
**	AirBarOffset (Point)
**
**		Offset to the upper-left corner of the air bar.
**
**	ModeTextPos (Point)
**
**		Relative position of the sunglass mode text display.
**
**
**
**	DamageBasePos (Point)
**
**		Relative position (upper-left corner) of the damage display.
**
**	DamageIconSize (int)
**		
**		Height and Width of the icons displaying while taking damage.
**
**	ActivationTextPos (Point)
**
**		Position of activation text
**
**	ActivationTextSize (int)
**
**		Font size of activation text
**
**	ActivationTextJustify (int)
**
**		Justification of activation text:
**			0 = Left, 1 = Center, 2 = Right
**
**	ActivationTextAlpha	(float)
**
**		Alpha of activation text (when activation is allowed)
**
**	ActivationTextColor (vector)
**
**		Color of activation text (when activation is allowed)
**
**	ActivationTextDisAlpha (float)
**
**		Alpha of activation text (when activation is not allowed)
**
**	ActivationTextDisColor (vector)
**
**		Alpha of activation text (when activation is not allowed)
**
*/

/*
** 	Hud#0 (Default) is Health=baronly; Armor=baronly; Ammo=icon,text,nobar;
*/

/************************************************************************
**
**	Miscellaneous Layout
**
** The Miscellaneous definition specifies data for interface components
** not covered under other definitions.
**
**	MessageBoxTexture (String)
**
**		The name of the DTX to use for background art on the Message Box.
**
**	DeathDelay (Float)
**
**		How long (in seconds) to wait after player dies before showing the Mission Failure screen.
**
**	SpyVisionModelColor (Vector)
**
**		Color to add to models while using spy vision <r, g, b>
**
**	SpyVisionScreenTint (Vector)
**
**		Color to tint the screen while using spy vision <r, g, b>
**
**	WeaponPickupColor (Vector)
**
**		Color to tint the screen when a weapon is picked up <r, g, b>
**
**	AmmoPickupColor (Vector)
**
**		Color to tint the screen when a weapon is picked up <r, g, b>
**
**	TintTime (Float)
**
**		Duration in seconds of screen tint when weapons or ammo are picked up
**
**	TimerFont (int)
**
**		Index of the font to use on screen timers.
**
**	TimerSize (int)
**
**		Size of the font to use on screen timers.
**
**	TimerPos (point)
**
**		Position at which to display the timer.
**
**
*/
